- [ ] **Step 3: Implement assembly**

`backend/throughline/review/assemble.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel

from throughline.game.model import SIDES, Game, Side
from throughline.plan.models import Draft, Plan
from throughline.review.checks import ItemCheck, check_item
from throughline.review.context import ReviewContext
from throughline.review.moments import Moment, detect_moments

GOLD_STEP = 30.0


class ReviewSummary(BaseModel):
    followed: int
    partly: int
    not_followed: int
    not_applicable: int


class ReviewBody(BaseModel):
    plan_id: str
    game_id: str
    us: Side
    teams: dict[Side, str]
    checks: list[ItemCheck]
    moments: list[Moment]
    summary: ReviewSummary
    gold: list[tuple[float, int]]


class EntryState(BaseModel):
    status: Literal["confirmed", "dismissed"] | None = None
    note: str = ""


def side_for_draft(game: Game, draft: Draft) -> Side | None:
    ours = sorted(draft.ours().values())
    for side in SIDES:
        if sorted(game.champions(side).values()) == ours:
            return side
    return None


def build_review(plan_id: str, plan: Plan, game: Game) -> ReviewBody:
    us = side_for_draft(game, plan.draft)
    if us is None:
        raise ValueError("this game was not played with the plan's draft")
    ctx = ReviewContext.build(game, us)
    checks = [check_item(item, ctx) for item in plan.items]
    counts = Counter(c.status for c in checks)
    gold: list[tuple[float, int]] = []
    t = 0.0
    while t <= game.duration:
        gold.append((t, ctx.index.gold_diff(us, t)))
        t += GOLD_STEP
    return ReviewBody(
        plan_id=plan_id, game_id=game.meta.game_id, us=us, teams=game.meta.teams, checks=checks,
        moments=detect_moments(ctx), gold=gold,
        summary=ReviewSummary(followed=counts["followed"], partly=counts["partly"],
                              not_followed=counts["not_followed"], not_applicable=counts["not_applicable"]),
    )
```

In `backend/throughline/store/app.py`, extend `SCHEMA`:

```sql

  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, edit_token TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, body TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entries (
  review_id TEXT NOT NULL, entry_id TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT, note TEXT NOT NULL,

```

and add:

```python
    def create_review(self, body: ReviewBody) -> tuple[str, str]:
        review_id, token = self._new_id(), secrets.token_urlsafe(18)
        with self._lock, self._con:
            self._con.execute("INSERT INTO reviews VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                              (review_id, token, self._now(), body.model_dump_json()))
        return review_id, token

    def get_review(self, review_id: str) -> ReviewBody | None:
        with self._lock:
            row = self._con.execute("SELECT body FROM reviews WHERE id = ?", (review_id,)).fetchone()
        return ReviewBody.model_validate_json(row[0]) if row else None

    def set_entry(self, review_id: str, token: str, entry_id: str, state: EntryState) -> bool:
        with self._lock, self._con:
            ok = self._con.execute("SELECT 1 FROM reviews WHERE id = ? AND edit_token = ?",
                                   (review_id, token)).fetchone()
            if not ok:
                return False
            self._con.execute(
                "INSERT INTO entries VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) "
                "ON CONFLICT (review_id, entry_id) DO UPDATE SET status = excluded.status, note = excluded.note",
                (review_id, entry_id, state.status, state.note))
        return True

    def entries(self, review_id: str) -> dict[str, EntryState]:
        with self._lock:
            rows = self._con.execute("SELECT entry_id, status, note FROM entries WHERE review_id = ?",
                                     (review_id,)).fetchall()
        return {entry_id: EntryState(status=status, note=note) for entry_id, status, note in rows}
```

with `from throughline.review.assemble import EntryState, ReviewBody`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Routes**

`backend/throughline/api/routes_reviews.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Header, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel

from throughline.api.routes_games import analytics
from throughline.api.routes_plans import app_store
from throughline.review.assemble import EntryState, ReviewBody, build_review

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api")


class CreateReview(BaseModel):
    plan_id: str
    game_id: str


def _entries_json(request: Request, review_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {k: v.model_dump() for k, v in app_store(request).entries(review_id).items()}


def _get(request: Request, review_id: str) -> ReviewBody:
    review = app_store(request).get_review(review_id)
    if review is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="review not found")
    return review


@router.post("/reviews")
def create_review(request: Request, body: CreateReview) -> dict[str, Any]:
    plan = app_store(request).get_plan(body.plan_id)
    if plan is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="plan not found")
    try:
        game = analytics(request).load_game(body.game_id)
    except KeyError as error:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="game not found") from error
    try:
        review = build_review(body.plan_id, plan, game)
    except ValueError as error:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(error)) from error
    review_id, token = app_store(request).create_review(review)
    return {"id": review_id, "edit_token": token, "review": review.model_dump(mode="json"), "entries": {}}


@router.get("/reviews/{review_id}")
def get_review(request: Request, review_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    review = _get(request, review_id)
    return {"id": review_id, "review": review.model_dump(mode="json"), "entries": _entries_json(request, review_id)}


@router.patch("/reviews/{review_id}/entries/{entry_id}")
def set_entry(request: Request, review_id: str, entry_id: str, state: EntryState,
              x_edit_token: str = Header(default="")) -> dict[str, Any]:
    review = _get(request, review_id)
    known = {c.item_id for c in review.checks} | {m.id for m in review.moments}
    if entry_id not in known:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="no such entry in this review")
    if not app_store(request).set_entry(review_id, x_edit_token, entry_id, state):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="this link cannot edit the review")
    return {"entries": _entries_json(request, review_id)}
```

Register it in `create_app`: `app.include_router(routes_reviews.router)`.

```ts
export type CheckStatus = "followed" | "partly" | "not_followed" | "not_applicable";

  id: string;
  kind: "objective_setup" | "unused_advantage" | "cross_map_trade" | "caught" | "gold_swing";
  t: number;
  verdict: "good" | "bad" | "neutral";

}

  game_id: string;
  us: Side;
  teams: Record<Side, string>;
```

`frontend/src/lib/reviewEntries.ts`:

```ts
import type { CheckStatus, Plan, ReviewBody } from "../types";

export type Tone = "good" | "bad" | "neutral" | "na";

export interface ReviewEntry {
  id: string;
  kind: "check" | "moment";
  t: number;
  label: string;
  text: string;
  tone: Tone;
  clip: [number, number];
  status?: CheckStatus;
}

const CHECK_TONE: Record<CheckStatus, Tone> = {
  followed: "good", partly: "neutral", not_followed: "bad", not_applicable: "na",
};

const MOMENT_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  objective_setup: "Objective setup",
  unused_advantage: "Advantage",
  cross_map_trade: "Cross-map trade",
  caught: "Caught out",
  gold_swing: "Gold swing",
};

export function reviewEntries(review: ReviewBody, plan?: Plan): ReviewEntry[] {
  const text = new Map(plan?.items.map((i) => [i.id, i.text]) ?? []);
  const checks: ReviewEntry[] = review.checks.map((c) => ({
    id: c.item_id, kind: "check", t: c.at, label: text.get(c.item_id) ?? c.item_id, text: c.numbers,
    tone: CHECK_TONE[c.status], clip: c.clip, status: c.status,
  }));
  const moments: ReviewEntry[] = review.moments.map((m) => ({
    id: m.id, kind: "moment", t: m.t, label: MOMENT_LABEL[m.kind] ?? m.kind, text: m.text,
    tone: m.verdict, clip: m.clip,
  }));
  const timed = [...checks, ...moments].filter((e) => e.tone !== "na").sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);
  return [...timed, ...checks.filter((e) => e.tone === "na")];
}

export function summaryLine(review: ReviewBody): string {
  const { followed, partly, not_followed } = review.summary;
  const applicable = followed + partly + not_followed;
  return `Plan followed ${followed} of ${applicable}${partly ? ` (${partly} partly)` : ""}`;
}
```

`frontend/src/components/GameTimeline.tsx`:

```tsx
import { fmtTime } from "../lib/time";
import type { ReviewEntry } from "../lib/reviewEntries";

const W = 640;
const H = 120;
const TONE_FILL = { good: "fill-emerald-400", bad: "fill-rose-400", neutral: "fill-zinc-300", na: "fill-zinc-600" } as const;

interface Props {
  gold: [number, number][];
  duration: number;
  entries: ReviewEntry[];
  selected?: string;
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
}

export function GameTimeline({ gold, duration, entries, selected, onSelect }: Props) {
  const peak = Math.max(1000, ...gold.map(([, g]) => Math.abs(g)));
  const x = (t: number) => (t / Math.max(duration, 1)) * W;
  const y = (g: number) => H / 2 - (g / peak) * (H / 2 - 8);
  const path = gold.map(([t, g], i) => `${i ? "L" : "M"}${x(t).toFixed(1)},${y(g).toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
  return (
    <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H + 18}`} className="w-full" role="img" aria-label="Gold difference over the game">
      <line x1={0} x2={W} y1={H / 2} y2={H / 2} className="stroke-zinc-700" />
      <path d={path} fill="none" className="stroke-zinc-200" strokeWidth={1.5} />
      {entries.filter((e) => e.tone !== "na").map((e) => (
        <circle key={e.id} cx={x(e.t)} cy={H - 6} r={selected === e.id ? 6 : 4}
          className={`${TONE_FILL[e.tone]} cursor-pointer`} onClick={() => onSelect(e.id)}>
          <title>{`${fmtTime(e.t)} ${e.label}`}</title>
        </circle>
      ))}
      {[0, duration / 2, duration].map((t) => (
        <text key={t} x={Math.min(x(t), W - 28)} y={H + 14} className="fill-zinc-500 text-[10px]">{fmtTime(t)}</text>
      ))}
    </svg>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/ReviewList.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { fmtTime } from "../lib/time";
import type { ReviewEntry } from "../lib/reviewEntries";
import type { EntryState } from "../types";

const BADGE = {
  good: "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300", bad: "bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300",
  neutral: "bg-zinc-500/15 text-zinc-300", na: "bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-500",
} as const;
const STATUS_TEXT = { followed: "Followed", partly: "Partly", not_followed: "Not followed", not_applicable: "Not applicable" };

interface Props {
  entries: ReviewEntry[];
  states: Record<string, EntryState>;
  selected?: string;
  editable: boolean;
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
  onState: (id: string, state: EntryState) => void;
}

export function ReviewList({ entries, states, selected, editable, onSelect, onState }: Props) {
  const [draftNote, setDraftNote] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  return (
    <ul className="space-y-2">
      {entries.map((e) => {
        const state = states[e.id] ?? { status: null, note: "" };
        const note = draftNote[e.id] ?? state.note;
        return (
          <li key={e.id}
            className={`rounded-lg border p-3 ${selected === e.id ? "border-zinc-400" : "border-zinc-800"} ${state.status === "dismissed" ? "opacity-50" : ""}`}>
            <button type="button" className="w-full text-left" onClick={() => onSelect(e.id)} disabled={e.tone === "na"}>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                <span className="tabular-nums text-zinc-500">{e.tone === "na" ? "--:--" : fmtTime(e.t)}</span>
                <span className={`rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 ${BADGE[e.tone]}`}>
                  {e.status ? STATUS_TEXT[e.status] : e.tone === "good" ? "Good" : e.tone === "bad" ? "Bad" : "Neutral"}
                </span>
                <span className="text-zinc-400">{e.kind === "check" ? "Plan" : e.label}</span>
              </div>
              {e.kind === "check" && <p className="mt-1 font-medium">{e.label}</p>}
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-300">{e.text}</p>
            </button>
            {(editable || state.note) && (
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                {editable && (["confirmed", "dismissed"] as const).map((s) => (
                  <button key={s} type="button"
                    className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 ${state.status === s ? "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-900" : "bg-zinc-800"}`}
                    onClick={() => onState(e.id, { status: state.status === s ? null : s, note: state.note })}>
                    {s === "confirmed" ? "Confirm" : "Dismiss"}
                  </button>
                ))}
                {editable ? (
                  <input value={note} placeholder="Note for the players"
                    onChange={(ev) => setDraftNote({ ...draftNote, [e.id]: ev.target.value })}
                    onBlur={() => note !== state.note && onState(e.id, { status: state.status, note })}
                    className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded bg-zinc-950 px-2 py-0.5" />
                ) : (
                  <p className="text-zinc-400">Coach: {state.note}</p>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/pages/ReviewPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { api } from "../api";
import { GameTimeline } from "../components/GameTimeline";
import { ReviewList } from "../components/ReviewList";
import { reviewEntries, summaryLine } from "../lib/reviewEntries";
import { getToken, takeTokenFromHash } from "../lib/tokens";
import { ReplayPlayer } from "../replay/ReplayPlayer";
import type { EntryState, ReviewResponse } from "../types";

export function ReviewPage() {
  const { id = "" } = useParams();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  useEffect(() => takeTokenFromHash("review", id), [id]);
  const token = getToken("review", id);
  const { data, error } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["review", id], queryFn: () => api.review(id) });
  const planId = data?.review.plan_id;
  const { data: plan } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["plan", planId], queryFn: () => api.plan(planId!), enabled: Boolean(planId) });
  const entries = useMemo(() => (data ? reviewEntries(data.review, plan?.plan) : []), [data, plan]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string>();
  const current = entries.find((e) => e.id === selected) ?? entries.find((e) => e.tone !== "na");
  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ entryId, state }: { entryId: string; state: EntryState }) => api.setEntry(id, token ?? "", entryId, state),
    onSuccess: (res) => queryClient.setQueryData<ReviewResponse>(["review", id], (old) => old && { ...old, entries: res.entries }),
  });

  if (error) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 text-rose-400">Review not found.</main>;
  if (!data) return <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8 text-zinc-500">Loading the review...</main>;
  const { review } = data;
  const duration = review.gold.length ? review.gold[review.gold.length - 1][0] : 0;

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{summaryLine(review)}</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-500">
            {review.teams[review.us]} ({review.us}) vs {review.teams[review.us === "blue" ? "red" : "blue"]} ·{" "}
            <Link to={`/plan/${review.plan_id}`} className="underline">the plan</Link>
          </p>
        </div>
        {token && (
          <button type="button" className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-1 text-sm"
            onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(`${window.location.origin}/review/${id}`)}>
            Copy link for players
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <section className="mt-6">
        <GameTimeline gold={review.gold} duration={duration} entries={entries} selected={current?.id} onSelect={setSelected} />
      </section>
      <div className="mt-6 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,640px)]">
        <ReviewList entries={entries} states={data.entries} selected={current?.id} editable={Boolean(token)}
          onSelect={setSelected} onState={(entryId, state) => save.mutate({ entryId, state })} />
        <div className="lg:sticky lg:top-4 lg:self-start">
          {current && (
            <ReplayPlayer key={current.id} gameId={review.game_id} start={current.clip[0]} end={current.clip[1]}
              markers={entries.filter((e) => e.tone !== "na").map((e) => ({
                t: e.t, label: e.label, tone: e.tone === "na" ? "neutral" : e.tone,
              }))} />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/components/ReviewAGame.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { api } from "../api";
import { saveToken } from "../lib/tokens";

export function ReviewAGame({ planId, suggestedGameId }: { planId: string; suggestedGameId?: string }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data: games = [] } = useQuery({ queryKey: ["plan-games", planId], queryFn: () => api.planGames(planId) });
  const create = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (gameId: string) => api.createReview(planId, gameId),
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      if (res.edit_token) saveToken("review", res.id, res.edit_token);
      navigate(`/review/${res.id}`);
    },
  });
  const sorted = [...games].sort((a, b) => Number(b.game_id === suggestedGameId) - Number(a.game_id === suggestedGameId));
  return (
    <section className="mt-10">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Review a game</h2>
      {sorted.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">No game in the dataset was played with this draft yet.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-3 divide-y divide-zinc-800">
          {sorted.map((g) => (
            <li key={g.game_id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 py-2 text-sm">
              <span>{g.teams.blue} vs {g.teams.red} · {g.tournament} · {new Date(g.started_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
              <button type="button" disabled={create.isPending} onClick={() => create.mutate(g.game_id)}
                className="rounded bg-emerald-500 px-3 py-1 font-medium text-zinc-950 disabled:opacity-40">
                Review
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {create.error && <p className="mt-2 text-rose-400">{String(create.error.message)}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
```

In `PlanPage.tsx`, read `?game=` with `useSearchParams` and render `<ReviewAGame planId={id} suggestedGameId={game ?? undefined} />` after the items. In `App.tsx`, add `<Route path="/review/:id" element={<ReviewPage />} />`.

### Task 23: Validation backtest

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/throughline/validation/__init__.py`, `backend/throughline/validation/backtest.py`
- Modify: `backend/throughline/cli.py` (add `validate`), `backend/throughline/api/routes_reviews.py` (add `GET /api/validation`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `build_plan`, `KnowledgeBase`/`KnowledgeFactory`, `ReviewContext`, `check_item`, `item_time`, `AnalyticsStore`.
- Produces:
  - `MIN_PRIOR_GAMES = 200`, `GD_EDGES = (-1500, -500, 500, 1500)`.
  - `SideResult(game_id, started_at, side, won, adherence: float | None, items: int)`, `CheckRow(game_id, started_at, side, won, item_type, status, gd_at)` (frozen dataclasses).
  - `adherence(statuses: list[str]) -> float | None`.
  - `run_backtest(store, knowledge: KnowledgeFactory = KnowledgeBase, min_prior_games: int = MIN_PRIOR_GAMES) -> tuple[list[SideResult], list[CheckRow]]`.
  - `summarize(sides, checks, holdout_from: datetime) -> dict[str, Any]` with keys `holdout_from`, `sides`, `adherence_quartiles` (list of `{low, high, sides, win_rate}`), `by_type` (list of `{type, followed, not_followed, followed_win_rate, not_followed_win_rate, adjusted_difference}`).
  - CLI: `throughline validate --holdout-from 2026-06-01`; writes `data/validation.json`.
  - Route: `GET /api/validation`.

```python
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from tests.plan.fake_knowledge import FakeKnowledge
from throughline.game.builder import DEFAULT_CHAMPIONS, GameBuilder
from throughline.store.analytics import AnalyticsStore
from throughline.validation.backtest import CheckRow, SideResult, adherence, run_backtest, summarize

T0 = datetime(2026, 9, 1, tzinfo=UTC)


def test_adherence() -> None:
    assert adherence(["followed", "partly", "not_followed", "not_applicable"]) == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert adherence(["not_applicable"]) is None


def test_backtest_only_reads_the_past(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    store = AnalyticsStore(tmp_path / "a.duckdb")
    store.init_schema()
    for n in range(4):
        store.write_game(GameBuilder(duration=900.0, game_id=f"g{n}", started_at=T0 + timedelta(days=n)).build())
    seen: list[datetime | None] = []

    def knowledge(s: AnalyticsStore, patch: str, before: datetime | None) -> FakeKnowledge:
        seen.append(before)
        return FakeKnowledge(edges={DEFAULT_CHAMPIONS["blue"][3]: 300.0})

    sides, checks = run_backtest(store, knowledge=knowledge, min_prior_games=2)
    assert seen == [T0 + timedelta(days=2), T0 + timedelta(days=3)]
    assert {(s.game_id, s.side) for s in sides} == {("g2", "blue"), ("g2", "red"), ("g3", "blue"), ("g3", "red")}
    assert all(c.game_id in {"g2", "g3"} for c in checks)


def test_summarize_quartiles_and_adjusted_difference() -> None:
    late = T0 + timedelta(days=100)
    sides = [SideResult(f"g{n}", late, "blue", n % 2 == 0, n / 10, 4) for n in range(10)]
    checks = (
        [CheckRow(f"f{n}", late, "blue", True, "lane", "followed", 0) for n in range(6)]
        + [CheckRow(f"f{n}", late, "blue", False, "lane", "followed", 0) for n in range(6, 10)]
        + [CheckRow(f"n{n}", late, "blue", True, "lane", "not_followed", 0) for n in range(4)]
        + [CheckRow(f"n{n}", late, "blue", False, "lane", "not_followed", 0) for n in range(4, 10)]
        + [CheckRow("early", T0, "blue", True, "lane", "followed", 0)]
    )
    report = summarize(sides, checks, holdout_from=T0 + timedelta(days=50))
    assert report["sides"] == 10
    assert sum(q["sides"] for q in report["adherence_quartiles"]) == 10
    lane = next(t for t in report["by_type"] if t["type"] == "lane")
    assert (lane["followed"], lane["not_followed"]) == (10, 10)
    assert lane["followed_win_rate"] == pytest.approx(0.6)
    assert lane["not_followed_win_rate"] == pytest.approx(0.4)
    assert lane["adjusted_difference"] == pytest.approx(0.2)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`backend/throughline/validation/__init__.py`: empty.

`backend/throughline/validation/backtest.py`:

```python
"""Does following the plan go with winning? A strict time-split backtest over the whole dataset."""

from __future__ import annotations

from bisect import bisect_right
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from statistics import quantiles
from typing import Any

from throughline.api.routes_plans import KnowledgeFactory
from throughline.game.model import SIDES, Side
from throughline.knowledge.base import KnowledgeBase
from throughline.plan.generator import build_plan
from throughline.plan.models import Draft, item_time
from throughline.review.checks import check_item
from throughline.review.context import ReviewContext
from throughline.store.analytics import AnalyticsStore

MIN_PRIOR_GAMES = 200
GD_EDGES = (-1500, -500, 500, 1500)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SideResult:
    game_id: str
    started_at: datetime
    side: Side
    won: bool
    adherence: float | None
    items: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CheckRow:
    game_id: str
    started_at: datetime
    side: Side
    won: bool
    item_type: str
    status: str
    gd_at: int


def adherence(statuses: list[str]) -> float | None:
    applicable = [s for s in statuses if s != "not_applicable"]
    if not applicable:
        return None
    return sum(1.0 if s == "followed" else 0.5 if s == "partly" else 0.0 for s in applicable) / len(applicable)


def run_backtest(store: AnalyticsStore, knowledge: KnowledgeFactory = KnowledgeBase,
                 min_prior_games: int = MIN_PRIOR_GAMES) -> tuple[list[SideResult], list[CheckRow]]:
    sides: list[SideResult] = []
    rows: list[CheckRow] = []
    for n, game_id in enumerate(store.game_ids_by_time()):
        if n < min_prior_games:
            continue
        game = store.load_game(game_id)
        kb = knowledge(store, game.patch, game.meta.started_at)
        for side in SIDES:
            draft = Draft(blue=game.champions("blue"), red=game.champions("red"), us=side, patch=game.patch)
            plan = build_plan(kb, draft)
            ctx = ReviewContext.build(game, side)
            checks = [check_item(item, ctx) for item in plan.items]
            won = game.winner == side
            sides.append(SideResult(game_id, game.meta.started_at, side, won,
                                    adherence([c.status for c in checks]), len(checks)))
            for item, check in zip(plan.items, checks, strict=True):
                rows.append(CheckRow(game_id, game.meta.started_at, side, won, item.type, check.status,
                                     ctx.index.gold_diff(side, item_time(item))))
    return sides, rows


def _rate(rows: list[CheckRow]) -> float | None:
    return sum(r.won for r in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None


def summarize(sides: list[SideResult], checks: list[CheckRow], holdout_from: datetime) -> dict[str, Any]:
    held = [s for s in sides if s.started_at >= holdout_from and s.adherence is not None]
    values = sorted(s.adherence for s in held if s.adherence is not None)
    quartiles_out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    if len(values) >= 4:
        edges = quantiles(values, n=4)
        buckets: dict[int, list[SideResult]] = defaultdict(list)
        for s in held:
            assert s.adherence is not None
            buckets[bisect_right(edges, s.adherence) if s.adherence < values[-1] else 3].append(s)
        bounds = [values[0], *edges, values[-1]]
        for q in range(4):
            group = buckets.get(q, [])
            quartiles_out.append({
                "low": bounds[q], "high": bounds[q + 1], "sides": len(group),
                "win_rate": sum(s.won for s in group) / len(group) if group else None,
            })

    eval_checks = [c for c in checks if c.started_at >= holdout_from]
    by_type: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item_type in sorted({c.item_type for c in eval_checks}):
        of_type = [c for c in eval_checks if c.item_type == item_type]
        followed = [c for c in of_type if c.status == "followed"]
        not_followed = [c for c in of_type if c.status == "not_followed"]
        weighted, weight = 0.0, 0
        for b in range(len(GD_EDGES) + 1):
            f = [c for c in followed if bisect_right(GD_EDGES, c.gd_at) == b]
            nf = [c for c in not_followed if bisect_right(GD_EDGES, c.gd_at) == b]
            w = min(len(f), len(nf))
            if w:
                rf, rn = _rate(f), _rate(nf)
                assert rf is not None and rn is not None
                weighted += w * (rf - rn)
                weight += w
        by_type.append({
            "type": item_type, "followed": len(followed), "not_followed": len(not_followed),
            "followed_win_rate": _rate(followed), "not_followed_win_rate": _rate(not_followed),
            "adjusted_difference": weighted / weight if weight else None,
        })
    return {"holdout_from": holdout_from.isoformat(), "sides": len(held), "adherence_quartiles": quartiles_out,
            "by_type": by_type}
```

Note: `validation` imports `KnowledgeFactory` from `api.routes_plans`; if that creates an import cycle, move `KnowledgeFactory` to `throughline/knowledge/base.py` and import it from there in both places.

Add to `backend/throughline/cli.py`:

```python
import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime

from throughline.validation.backtest import run_backtest, summarize


@app.command()
def validate(holdout_from: str = "2026-06-01") -> None:
    """Backtest plans against games, using only earlier games for each plan, and write data/validation.json."""
    settings = Settings.from_env()
    store = AnalyticsStore(settings.analytics_db)
    sides, checks = run_backtest(store)
    report = summarize(sides, checks, datetime.fromisoformat(holdout_from).replace(tzinfo=UTC))
    out = settings.data_dir / "validation.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    typer.echo(f"wrote {out}: {report['sides']} sides in the holdout")
```

Add to `backend/throughline/api/routes_reviews.py`:

```python
import json


@router.get("/validation")
def validation(request: Request) -> dict[str, Any]:
    path = request.app.state.settings.data_dir / "validation.json"
    if not path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="no validation report yet")
    report: dict[str, Any] = json.loads(path.read_text())
    return report
```

### Task 24: Synthetic dataset, static frontend and deployment

Anyone without the GRID starter data can run everything on synthetic games; the hosted demo serves the built frontend from the API.
The starter data's license allows it only on the participant's machine, so the hosted demo runs the synthetic league unless the organizers give written permission to host the real data (see Step 8).

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/throughline/synthetic.py`, `Dockerfile`, `scripts/start.sh`, `.dockerignore`
- Modify: `backend/throughline/cli.py` (`seed`, `build --offline`), `backend/throughline/api/app.py` (static frontend)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_synthetic.py`, `backend/tests/api/test_static.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `synthetic_games(n: int, seed: int = 7) -> Iterator[Game]`, `OFFLINE_LEGENDARY: set[str]`; CLI `throughline seed --games 300`, `throughline build --offline`; `create_app` serving `settings.frontend_dist` with an SPA fallback for non-`/api` paths.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the synthetic dataset**

`backend/throughline/synthetic.py`:

```python
"""A seeded synthetic league for anyone without the GRID starter data, end-to-end tests and offline work.

Each champion has a hidden early and late strength; gold leads, objectives and results follow them with noise,
so the knowledge base finds real (synthetic) patterns.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import random
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

from throughline.game.builder import GameBuilder
from throughline.game.geometry import FOUNTAIN, PITS
from throughline.game.model import ROLES, SIDES, Game, Side, other

OFFLINE_LEGENDARY = {"Infinity Edge", "Rabadon's Deathcap", "Black Cleaver", "Zhonya's Hourglass"}
POOLS: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    "top": ["Aatrox", "Gnar", "Jax", "Renekton", "Rumble", "KSante", "Ornn", "Jayce"],
    "jungle": ["Vi", "Sejuani", "LeeSin", "Maokai", "Xinzhao", "Viego", "Wukong", "Nidalee"],
    "mid": ["Ahri", "Orianna", "Azir", "Syndra", "Taliyah", "Yone", "Sylas", "Corki"],
    "bot": ["Ezreal", "Kaisa", "Varus", "Xayah", "Jinx", "Aphelios", "Zeri", "Kalista"],
    "support": ["Rakan", "Nautilus", "Alistar", "Rell", "Braum", "Leona", "Renata", "Bard"],
}
T0 = datetime(2026, 6, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
LANE_SPOT = {"top": (1500.0, 12500.0), "mid": (7500.0, 7500.0), "bot": (12500.0, 1500.0)}


def synthetic_games(n: int, seed: int = 7) -> Iterator[Game]:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    strength = {c: (rng.gauss(0, 1), rng.gauss(0, 1)) for pool in POOLS.values() for c in pool}
    for i in range(n):
        picks = {role: rng.sample(POOLS[role], 2) for role in ROLES}
        champions: dict[Side, list[str]] = {"blue": [picks[r][0] for r in ROLES], "red": [picks[r][1] for r in ROLES]}
        early = sum(strength[c][0] for c in champions["blue"]) - sum(strength[c][0] for c in champions["red"])
        late = sum(strength[c][1] for c in champions["blue"]) - sum(strength[c][1] for c in champions["red"])
        early += rng.gauss(0, 1.5)
        late += rng.gauss(0, 1.5)
        winner: Side = "blue" if early * 0.4 + late * 0.6 > 0 else "red"
        early_side: Side = "blue" if early > 0 else "red"
        duration = float(rng.randint(1560, 2280))
        b = GameBuilder(duration=duration, winner=winner, patch=rng.choice(["15.21", "15.22", "15.23"]),
                        started_at=T0 + timedelta(hours=8 * i), champions=champions, game_id=f"syn{i:04d}-1",
                        tournament="Synthetic League")
        b.gold_lead(early_side, int(abs(early) * 450), 120.0, 900.0)
        b.gold_lead(winner, int(1500 + abs(late) * 900), 900.0, duration - 60.0)

        dragon_side = early_side if rng.random() < 0.7 else other(early_side)
        pids = range(1, 6) if dragon_side == "blue" else range(6, 11)
        for pid in list(pids)[1:4]:
            b.walk(pid, 230.0, 285.0, FOUNTAIN[dragon_side], PITS["dragon"])
        b.spawn(294.0, "dragon", *PITS["dragon"])
        b.spawn(473.0, "grubs", *PITS["baron"])
        b.spawn(897.0, "herald", *PITS["baron"])
        b.spawn(1480.0, "baron", *PITS["baron"])
        b.objective(300.0 + rng.uniform(15, 60), "dragon", dragon_side)
        b.objective(480.0 + rng.uniform(20, 90), "grubs", early_side if rng.random() < 0.6 else other(early_side))
        b.objective(900.0 + rng.uniform(20, 90), "herald", rng.choice(SIDES))
        b.objective(1500.0 + rng.uniform(30, 200), "baron", winner)

        b.plate(rng.uniform(300, 840), "mid", early_side)
        for lane in ("top", "mid", "bot"):
            for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 3)):
                b.plate(rng.uniform(300, 840), lane, early_side if rng.random() < 0.65 else other(early_side))
        b.structure(rng.uniform(800, 1100), "turret", "bot", early_side)
        b.structure(rng.uniform(1300, 1700), "turret", "top", winner)

        for _ in range(rng.randint(6, 14)):
            t = rng.uniform(180, duration - 30)
            lane = rng.choice(["top", "mid", "bot"])
            x, y = LANE_SPOT[lane]
            scorer = winner if (t > 900 and rng.random() < 0.65) or (t <= 900 and rng.random() < 0.5) else other(winner)
            killer = rng.randint(1, 5) if scorer == "blue" else rng.randint(6, 10)
            victim = rng.randint(6, 10) if scorer == "blue" else rng.randint(1, 5)
            b.kill(t, killer, victim, x + rng.uniform(-400, 400), y + rng.uniform(-400, 400))

        for pid in range(1, 11):
            b.item(rng.uniform(720, 960), pid, rng.choice(sorted(OFFLINE_LEGENDARY)))
        yield b.build()
```

Add to `backend/throughline/cli.py`:

```python
from throughline.synthetic import OFFLINE_LEGENDARY, synthetic_games


@app.command()
def seed(games: int = 300) -> None:
    """Fill the analytics database with a synthetic league (no GRID data needed)."""
    settings = Settings.from_env()
    store = AnalyticsStore(settings.analytics_db)
    store.init_schema()
    for n, game in enumerate(synthetic_games(games), start=1):
        store.write_game(game)
        if n % 50 == 0 or n == games:
            typer.echo(f"{n}/{games} games")
```

Change `build` so it can run without network access:

```python
@app.command()
def build(offline: bool = False) -> None:
    """Compute participant features for every stored game. --offline skips Data Dragon (synthetic data)."""
    settings = Settings.from_env()
    store = AnalyticsStore(settings.analytics_db)
    store.init_schema()
    if offline:
        legendary = OFFLINE_LEGENDARY
    else:
        legendary = legendary_item_names(fetch_item_jsons(_patches(store), settings.data_dir / "ddragon"))
    ids = store.game_ids_by_time()
    for n, game_id in enumerate(ids, start=1):
        store.write_features(game_id, participant_features(store.load_game(game_id), legendary))
        if n % 50 == 0 or n == len(ids):
            typer.echo(f"{n}/{len(ids)} games")
```

```python
    if settings.frontend_dist is not None:
        dist = settings.frontend_dist.resolve()
        app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory=dist / "assets"), name="assets")

        @app.get("/{path:path}", include_in_schema=False)
        def spa(path: str) -> FileResponse:
            if path.startswith("api/"):
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404)
            candidate = dist / path
            if path and candidate.is_file() and dist in candidate.resolve().parents:
                return FileResponse(candidate)
            return FileResponse(dist / "index.html")
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Container and start script**

`Dockerfile`:

```dockerfile
FROM node:24.22.3-alpine AS web
WORKDIR /web
COPY frontend/package.json frontend/package-lock.json ./
RUN npm ci
COPY frontend/ ./
RUN npm run build

FROM python:3.12.8-slim
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends git && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
COPY --from=ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:0.5.11 /uv /usr/local/bin/uv
WORKDIR /app/backend
COPY backend/pyproject.toml backend/uv.lock ./
RUN uv sync --frozen --no-dev --no-install-project
COPY backend/ ./
RUN uv sync --frozen --no-dev
COPY --from=web /web/dist /app/frontend/dist
COPY scripts/start.sh /app/start.sh
ENV TL_DATA_DIR=/data STL_DATA_DIR=/data/stl TL_FRONTEND_DIST=/app/frontend/dist PATH="/app/backend/.venv/bin:$PATH"
EXPOSE 8000
CMD ["/app/start.sh"]
```

`scripts/start.sh` (mark executable):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
DB="${TL_ANALYTICS_DB:-$TL_DATA_DIR/throughline.duckdb}"
# The real data only with the organizers' written permission to host it: TL_REAL_DATA=1.
if [ "${TL_REAL_DATA:-0}" = "1" ] && [ ! -f "$STL_DATA_DIR/lol/series.csv" ]; then
  git clone --filter=blob:none --sparse https://github.com/cloud9esports/stl-hackathon-2026-grid-starter.git "$STL_DATA_DIR"
  git -C "$STL_DATA_DIR" sparse-checkout set lol meta
fi
if [ ! -f "$DB" ]; then
  if [ "${TL_REAL_DATA:-0}" = "1" ]; then
    throughline ingest
    throughline build
  else
    throughline seed --games 400
    throughline build --offline
  fi
  throughline validate --holdout-from "${TL_HOLDOUT_FROM:-2026-08-01}"
fi
exec uvicorn throughline.api.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port "${PORT:-8000}"
```

`.dockerignore`: `data/`, `data-synthetic/`, `**/node_modules`, `**/.venv`, `frontend/dist`, `.git`.

Run: `docker build -t throughline . && docker run --rm -p 8000:8000 -v "$PWD/data-docker:/data" throughline`
Expected: seeds on first start, then serves the app at http://localhost:8000 with deep links (`/review/<id>`) working on reload.

- [ ] **Step 8: Deploy**

Create a Railway project from the repository (Dockerfile build) with a volume mounted at `/data` and the variable `TL_HOLDOUT_FROM` (`2025-08-20` for the real data).
Without `TL_REAL_DATA`, it serves the synthetic league; add a banner that says so, and record the real-data demo for the video from a local run.
Set `TL_REAL_DATA=1` only after the organizers confirm in writing that the starter data may be hosted for judging (the license allows it only on the participant's machine); the first boot then clones `lol/` and `meta/`, ingests and builds (minutes).
Check the deploy logs until uvicorn starts, then open the public URL and run through the core flow.

- [ ] **Step 9: Commit**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "feat: synthetic league, static frontend serving and container deployment"
```

### Task 25: End-to-end test and polish pass

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/playwright.config.ts`, `frontend/e2e/loop.spec.ts`, `scripts/e2e-backend.sh`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (e2e job), any UI file the polish pass touches

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the whole app on the synthetic league.

- [ ] **Step 1: Backend script for e2e**

`scripts/e2e-backend.sh` (executable):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
export TL_DATA_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
cd "$(dirname "$0")/../backend"
uv run throughline seed --games 260
uv run throughline build --offline
exec uv run uvicorn throughline.api.main:app --port 8000
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Playwright config and the test**

`frontend/playwright.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig } from "@playwright/test";

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "e2e",
  timeout: 60_000,
  use: { baseURL: "http://localhost:5173", trace: "retain-on-failure" },
  webServer: [
    { command: "../scripts/e2e-backend.sh", url: "http://localhost:8000/api/health", timeout: 240_000, reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI },
    { command: "npm run dev -- --port 5173", url: "http://localhost:5173", reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI },
  ],
});
```

`frontend/e2e/loop.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

test("draft to plan to review, with a clip and a note", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: /Plan as/ }).first().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Game plan" })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator("li", { hasText: "games" }).first()).toBeVisible();

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Review" }).first().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: /Plan followed \d+ of \d+/ })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator("canvas")).toBeVisible();

  const entry = page.locator("li").filter({ has: page.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirm" }) }).first();
  await entry.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirm" }).click();
  await entry.getByPlaceholder("Note for the players").fill("Watch the timing");
  await entry.getByPlaceholder("Note for the players").blur();
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByPlaceholder("Note for the players").first()).toHaveValue("Watch the timing");
});

test("a read-only review link shows notes but no controls", async ({ page, browser }) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.getByRole("link", { name: /Plan as/ }).first().click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Review" }).first().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { name: /Plan followed/ })).toBeVisible();
  const url = page.url();
  const other = await (await browser.newContext()).newPage();
  await other.goto(url);
  await expect(other.getByRole("heading", { name: /Plan followed/ })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(other.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirm" })).toHaveCount(0);
});
```

Add a CI job `e2e` (needs both setups, runs `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium` then `npm run e2e` in `frontend/`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Run it**

Run: `cd frontend && npx playwright install chromium && npm run e2e`
Expected: 2 passed. Fix any flakiness at its cause (a missing wait on data, not a longer timeout).

- [ ] **Step 4: Polish pass**

Go through every page at 1440, 1024 and 375 px wide, in the browser, against this list, and fix everything that fails:
- No horizontal scroll; nothing clipped; tap targets at least 32 px on phones.
- Loading, empty and error states on every page read as sentences, not blank space.
- Team colours: blue side `#3b82f6`, red side `#ef4444`, consistent in the draft strip, the replay and the lists.
- Numbers use tabular figures; times always `m:ss`; gold always signed with thousands separators.
- Keyboard: every control reachable and visibly focused.
- The footer notice is on every page.
Screenshot each page at desktop and phone width into `docs/screenshots/` for the README.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add -A
git commit -m "test(e2e): the plan-and-review loop; polish pass"
```

### Task 26: Submission materials

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/methodology.md`, `AI_SHOWCASE.md`, `docs/devpost.md`
- Modify: `README.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: README**

Sections, in this order, one sentence per line:
1. One-line pitch and a hero screenshot of the Review page.
2. What it does: Plan (draft in, checkable items out, with evidence and games counts) and Review (each item checked, macro moments, clips, coach notes), three screenshots.
3. Try it: the live URL; then local run with the synthetic league (`uv sync`, `throughline seed`, `throughline build --offline`, `uvicorn`, `npm run dev`), then with the GRID starter data (the sparse clone next to the repository from Phase 1, `throughline ingest`, `throughline build`, `throughline validate`).
4. How it works: the pipeline diagram from the spec, and a link to `docs/methodology.md`.
5. Built with JetBrains: PyCharm, WebStorm, DataGrip, Junie (link to `AI_SHOWCASE.md`, `.junie/AGENTS.md`, `docs/BUILD_LOG.md`).
6. Data: the hackathon's GRID starter data (not in this repository; its license limits it to the hackathon), Riot Data Dragon assets.
7. License (AGPL-3.0) and the Riot notice, verbatim.

- [ ] **Step 2: Methodology**

`docs/methodology.md`, sections:
1. The problem and the approach (from the spec).
2. Data: what GRID events are used, the census (`docs/data/events.md`), the dataset (leagues, splits, game count), and what the feed cannot give: positions only every few seconds (and how `GameIndex` handles gaps), no wards, no roles (and how they are inferred, with the hand check), and the clock offset from Task 3.
3. Knowledge base: tempo curves, shrinkage (`value * n / (n + 20)`), matchups (5+ games), spikes (10+ games), objective tendencies, patch windows and the fallback.
4. The plan: each item type, how it is built, its guard, and every constant from `plan/constants.py` with its value.
5. The review: each checker's rule and each moment detector's rule, with their constants.
6. Validation: the time split, the holdout date, the confounding by gold lead and how the bucketed comparison limits it, then the numbers from `data/validation.json` as tables, and what they do and do not show.
7. Hand checks: the VOD comparisons from BUILD_LOG, summarised (how many verdicts and moments were right).
8. Limits: sample sizes, patches, what the feed cannot show (minion waves, cooldowns, comms).

- [ ] **Step 3: AI_SHOWCASE.md**

Written from `docs/BUILD_LOG.md`:
1. Setup: which JetBrains tools, the `.junie/AGENTS.md` rules and why each exists.
2. Three or four concrete stories with screenshots, each: the task, the prompt, what Junie produced, what was wrong and how it was caught (usually a failing test or a VOD check), the fix.
3. What worked and what did not, honestly.
4. Numbers: commits and tasks done mostly by Junie, tests written test-first, time from spec to deploy.

- [ ] **Step 4: Devpost text and video**

`docs/devpost.md`: the submission text (inspiration, what it does, how we built it, challenges, accomplishments, what we learned, what is next), the JetBrains tools section, the links (repo, live demo, video, methodology, AI_SHOWCASE).

Record the video to the spec's script, under 3 minutes (aim for 2:50): no team logos, no copyrighted music, no broadcast footage; upload to YouTube as Public; check it plays logged out.

- [ ] **Step 5: Final checks and commit**

- The repository's About section shows the AGPL-3.0 license.
- The live demo works logged out, in a private window, on a phone.
- `docs/BUILD_LOG.md` has an entry for every task.
- CI is green on `main`.

```bash
git commit -m "docs: README, methodology, AI showcase and Devpost text"
```
